- **ralph-planner**: Phase-scoped task-decomposition agent for the bridge `plan` phase. - **ralph-playtest-interviewer**: Phase-scoped playtest-criteria clarification agent for the bridge `intake` phase (games / interactive apps). - **ratchet-adversary**: Ratchet adversarial reviewer. Assumes the checkpoint's code is wrong and tries to prove it — hunting for violations of the project's written standards (lens=standards) and for bugs, edge cases, security and data-integrity problems (lens=correctness), or both. Every finding cites a rule or a concrete failure scenario. Approves only by returning zero findings. Never fixes code. Spawned fresh each round by the ratchet-adversarial-loop. ([rahilp/Ratchet-Loop](https://github.com/rahilp/Ratchet-Loop/blob/HEAD/agents/ratchet-adversary.md) · ★4)
- **ratchet-app-scout**: Ratchet loop scout. Checks the current state of the app before a build — detects the stack, test runner, start command and URL, makes sure the app actually runs, finds login credentials/seed data for UI captures, and drafts .ratchet/standards.md from the codebase if no written standard exists. Spawned by the ratchet-orchestrator in parallel with the checkpoint planner. ([rahilp/Ratchet-Loop](https://github.com/rahilp/Ratchet-Loop/blob/HEAD/agents/ratchet-app-scout.md) · ★4)
- **ratchet-behavior-reviewer**: Ratchet UI-gate behaviour reviewer. Compares how the real app and the reference respond to the same interaction journey — step-by-step screenshots, accessibility trees, console errors and browser-only test cases — and lists every behavioural difference (wrong result, missing feedback, broken focus, validation, navigation, loading/empty/error states). Returns INVALID when the starting states don't match. Works only from an isolated INPUT.md folder; run by ui_review.py through a provider other than the host when one is available, otherwise as the host's own sub-agent. ([rahilp/Ratchet-Loop](https://github.com/rahilp/Ratchet-Loop/blob/HEAD/agents/ratchet-behavior-reviewer.md) · ★4)
- **ratchet-checkpoint-planner**: Ratchet checkpoint planner. Splits a large feature, screen migration or refactor into small ordered checkpoints of increasing complexity, each with plain-language "done when" criteria and a decision on which gates apply, written to checkpoints.json. PLAN mode creates the plan; EXTEND mode appends checkpoints from human feedback. Returns QUESTIONS instead of guessing when the request is ambiguous. Spawned by ratchet-orchestrator. ([rahilp/Ratchet-Loop](https://github.com/rahilp/Ratchet-Loop/blob/HEAD/agents/ratchet-checkpoint-planner.md) · ★4)
- **ratchet-critic**: HARNESS council critic — the ratchet. For each CONFIRMED root, it chooses the cheapest DETERMINISTIC output (new guardrail/ESLint Tier-1 · new pre-edit lesson Tier-2 · prune-revision of an existing lesson · a CLAUDE.md pointer proposal). If no deterministic output exists, it records "no actionable artifact + reason" — never inventing a change. It proposes; the librarian enacts. Read-only. ([SyniakSviatoslav/dowiz](https://github.com/SyniakSviatoslav/dowiz/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ratchet-critic.md) · ★2)
- **ratchet-design-system**: Ratchet design-system agent. Follows the ratchet-design-md skill. Spawned by ratchet-orchestrator before prototyping when no DESIGN.md exists, or directly when the user wants a design system file. ([rahilp/Ratchet-Loop](https://github.com/rahilp/Ratchet-Loop/blob/HEAD/agents/ratchet-design-system.md) · ★4)

- **ratchet-implementer**: Ratchet implementer. Never edits tests. Spawned by ratchet-orchestrator. ([rahilp/Ratchet-Loop](https://github.com/rahilp/Ratchet-Loop/blob/HEAD/agents/ratchet-implementer.md) · ★4)
- **ratchet-prototyper**: Ratchet design/prototype agent. - **ratchet-test-planner**: Ratchet test planner. - **ratchet-test-writer**: Ratchet test writer. Spawned by ratchet-orchestrator at the start of each checkpoint (sometimes several in parallel on different files). - **ratchet-variant-writer**: Ratchet variant writer. Distinct from safety-auditor (governance-memo compliance) and emancipatory-auditor (subject+emancipatory clause); this role audits *path*, not *clauses*. Spawn when the COO notices the shape in its own move and wants an external read before banking the action. ([coo-labs/skills](https://github.com/coo-labs/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/rationalization-discriminator.md))
- **ratsmitglied**: Ein Mitglied eines KI-Rats (z. B. Skill generalprobe). Liest seinen Auftrag aus einer Datei, urteilt nach Urteilsregeln und Dossier und antwortet ausschließlich mit dem verlangten JSON. Nur von einem Rats-Skill aufrufen lassen. ([Vektoris-AI/generalprobe](https://github.com/Vektoris-AI/generalprobe/blob/HEAD/agents/ratsmitglied.md))
- **rauch**: You are writing tests as Guillermo Rauch. Creator of Socket.io, Mongoose, Vercel/Next.js. Your expertise: real-time messaging, integration testing, API contracts, developer experience. ([neilobremski/bin](https://github.com/neilobremski/bin/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/rauch.md))
- **rauf-backlog-reviewer**: Delegate a second-opinion QA audit of a rauf backlog.json — coverage, scoping, dependency sanity, acceptance-criteria quality, enum correctness, and portability. Use when the user asks to review/QA/audit a rauf backlog and you want to hand the audit to a focused subagent. ([garygentry/rauf](https://github.com/garygentry/rauf/blob/HEAD/agents/rauf-backlog-reviewer.md) · 2 copies)
- **rauf-loop-driver**: Delegate operating the rauf CLI loop — the run → observe → recover lifecycle — to a focused subagent. Use when the user asks to run/supervise/recover a rauf loop and you want a subagent to drive the CLI and report status, rather than behaving as a loop iteration yourself. ([garygentry/rauf](https://github.com/garygentry/rauf/blob/HEAD/adapters/copilot/agents/rauf-loop-driver.agent.md) · 2 copies)

- **ravelry-api-dev**: Use this agent to add or modify a Ravelry API call end-to-end in FiberSocial, following the central-client pattern in RavelryApiClient.kt. Trigger examples: "add an endpoint to fetch a user's favorites", "wire up a POST to flag a post", "the app needs to leave an event — there's no JSON API, scrape the web form", "add pagination to getGroupTopics", "expose the topic-lock action". It owns the model → wrapper-DTO → suspend fun → repo/ViewModel → MockEngine-test chain and the web-protocol (scraping) alternative for actions the JSON API doesn't expose. ([torrey1028/FiberSocial](https://github.com/torrey1028/FiberSocial/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ravelry-api-dev.md))
- **raven**: Raven is the director's product-thinking colleague — the resident expert on the product's context library. She grounds every conversation in what the library actually records, thinks with you rather than just retrieving — brainstorming, pressure-testing, tracing implications, spotting gaps — and fronts the plays that build and improve the library. Use her to understand and improve the product. Examples: - "What's our strategy for onboarding?" - "I'm thinking about adding a sixth agent — poke holes in that" - "We just decided to support Cursor. What does that change?" - "What are we missing in… ([sociotechnica-org/alexandria](https://github.com/sociotechnica-org/alexandria/blob/HEAD/alexandria/agents/raven.md) · ★4 · 3 copies)
- **Raven**: Deep analysis specialist — hidden patterns, beneath-the-surface issues, architectural undercurrents ([tmcleod3/voidforge](https://github.com/tmcleod3/voidforge/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/raven-deep-analysis.md) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **raven**: Program thinking partner for Alexandria. The resident program expert — reads the knowledge graph, the signal queue, the feedback queue, the provenance log, and health reports to engage humans in program brainstorming, problem-solving, and decision-making. Processo invariante: spec antes de código, rastreabilidade RN, testes verdes (comando vem do playbook), relatório compacto. - **re-porter**: Reimplements traced and measured game behaviour in new code that behaves exactly like the original: same structure, numeric types, order of operations, random draw order, iteration order and integer semantics, citing the original function on every item. - **re-publisher**: Builds and keeps the project's website in sites/NAME/ (or site/): writes a live diary entry for each milestone, consolidates each past day into one update-notes post, adds a page for each new document through @include, keeps the front page's status true, and checks that nothing from the game is on it. Use after a milestone, a release, or when the documents in docs/ change shape. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/agents/re-publisher.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **re-qa-verifier**: Independent reverse-engineering QA gate (v3). Re-measures the rebuilt STEP against scan, calipers, and photos with its own registration (never trusting drafter numbers), audits Tier-0 surface quality, and writes an APPROVED/REVISE verdict into the part's repo folder. Mandatory final gate — feature-level review never replaces it. ([mikbalarikan/agentic_STL-to-CAD](https://github.com/mikbalarikan/agentic_STL-to-CAD/blob/HEAD/baseline/agents/re-qa-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **re-randomiser**: Finds every random number generator in a game, its state, its seeding and every caller, then measures the draw order with call traces so a port can draw the same numbers in the same order. Use before porting anything that draws random numbers. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/re-randomiser.agent.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **re-recon**: Phase 1 of a binary analysis. Identifies the target, builds the threat model, extracts every artefact, and hands the next phase a map. Run this FIRST and alone - every other RE agent reads what it produces. ([tihanyin/REx-skill](https://github.com/tihanyin/REx-skill/blob/HEAD/claude-skill/agents/re-recon.md) · ★125)
- **re-recon**: Read-only reconnaissance for a binary/CTF/malware target. Spawn this when recon means reading large dumps (full strings, disassembly, many source files) — it does the breadth-first sweep and returns a tight triage summary + recommended path, keeping the raw output out of the caller's context. Use it before deep analysis, not for the deep dive itself. ([TyrusRC/chimera](https://github.com/TyrusRC/chimera/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/re-recon.md) · ★3)
- **re-reconcile**: Final phase. Adjudicates the stance agents' findings against the code, produces the single reconciled deliverable and the written report. Run only after the stance agents have finished. ([tihanyin/REx-skill](https://github.com/tihanyin/REx-skill/blob/HEAD/claude-skill/agents/re-reconcile.md) · ★125)
- **re-refuter**: Adversarial checker. Give it ONE claim (a sentence of a facts page, or a verifier's verdict) or, in Page mode, a facts page; it re-runs the named verifier on the named capture itself, reads the cited instructions itself, and returns confirmed / refuted / irreproducible with the output pasted. Default in doubt: irreproducible. Never edits a file, never softens a refutation. ([JeanxPereira/CrystalOSD](https://github.com/JeanxPereira/CrystalOSD/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/re-refuter.md) · ★6)
- **re-review-coordinator**: Re-review Coordinator — classifies existing PR Threads into addressed/disputed/pending/obsolete/regressed, decides reply vs resolve vs reopen vs new-Thread per Finding, and emits a structured plan for the ADO Writer. ([unic/unic-agents-plugins](https://github.com/unic/unic-agents-plugins/blob/HEAD/apps/claude-code/unic-pr-review/agents/re-review-coordinator.md) · ★1)
- **re-review-coordinator**: Own the full re-review state machine: prior-thread detection, partial-run check, thread classification, finding matching, and reply posting to classified threads. Returns classification counts, fresh findings, and an earlyExit flag. ([unic/unic-agents-plugins](https://github.com/unic/unic-agents-plugins/blob/HEAD/apps/claude-code/pr-review/agents/re-review-coordinator.md) · ★1)
- **re-reviewer**: Verifies a fix round - verdicts each prior finding ADDRESSED or NOT ADDRESSED and inspects only the fix diff for new breakage. Not a fresh review; dispatch with the findings list and fix commit range. ([roundpilot/superpowers-antigravity](https://github.com/roundpilot/superpowers-antigravity/blob/HEAD/agents/re-reviewer.md) · ★92)
- **re-reviewer**: Use when an implementer has fixed review findings and the fix must be verified against exactly those findings, not reviewed again from scratch. ([Amangaron/leanpowers](https://github.com/Amangaron/leanpowers/blob/HEAD/agents/re-reviewer.md))
- **re-reviewer**: Station 3.2 Fix-Round Auditor. Verifies prior review findings with zero hand-waving and performs an unbiased fresh-eyes audit on the fix diff for new regressions (Derivative Flaws). READ-ONLY. ([andyandsmoothies-ship-it/vtcoon](https://github.com/andyandsmoothies-ship-it/vtcoon/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/re-reviewer.md))
- **re-reviewer**: Scoped re-review of ONE fix round — verdicts each prior blocking finding ADDRESSED / NOT_ADDRESSED / REFUTATION_ACCEPTED against the fix-only diff, adjudicates the fixer's refutations, and checks the fix diff itself for new breakage. Never a fresh full review. Dispatched by the slice-wave workflow after the implementer returns from fix mode. Read-only and advisory; never edits code. ([z2297/spec-loop-2](https://github.com/z2297/spec-loop-2/blob/HEAD/plugins/spec-loop/agents/re-reviewer.md))
- **re-revisor**: Re-revisa só o delta de uma rodada de correção de task, contra os achados que a motivaram. Não edita. ([Andred21/lotus](https://github.com/Andred21/lotus/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/re-revisor.md))

- **Re-run W8-1 smoke-test end-to-end (closes W7-1-CARRY-1)**: Verifies W9-1 + W9-2 fix the cascade. Closes W7-1-CARRY-1 if the real-run smoke-test now passes. ([crbazevedo/learning-to-anticipate-flexible-choices](https://github.com/crbazevedo/learning-to-anticipate-flexible-choices/blob/HEAD/.dfg/agents/W9-4-smoke-test-end-to-end-rerun.md) · ★1)
- **RE-Runtime-Tracer**: **Agent Type**: specialized-development **RE Level**: 3 (Dynamic Analysis) **Timebox**: ≤1 hour per binary **Slash Command**: `/re:dynamic` ([DNYoussef/context-cascade](https://github.com/DNYoussef/context-cascade/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/RE-Runtime-Tracer.md) · ★32)
- **re-safety**: Safety-proof stance: tries to discharge the obligation that the program is sound, and reports every obligation it cannot discharge. Run in parallel with re-bughunt, which must not see its output. ([tihanyin/REx-skill](https://github.com/tihanyin/REx-skill/blob/HEAD/claude-skill/agents/re-safety.md) · ★125)
- **re-scout**: Read-only scout for the PS2 OSD. Give it ONE question (a function, a variable, a value); it returns the HDD OSD 1.10U and ROM 2.30 addresses, the callers and writers, what the code computes (as read), and what a verifier would have to probe. Never writes code or facts; never says "does not exist", only "not found, and the search finds &lt;known thing&gt;". ([JeanxPereira/CrystalOSD](https://github.com/JeanxPereira/CrystalOSD/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/re-scout.md) · ★6)
- **re-scout**: Use when: searching and scraping real estate listings across multiple country-specific portals (Spain, Portugal, Germany, Australia, US, Cyprus). Use when: you want to find properties matching specific house types, features, price ranges, or wish-list criteria from top-rated sites. Use when: running automated multi-portal property discovery, comparing listings across platforms, or building a cross-portal watchlist. Use when: extracting structured listing data (price, location, rooms, features, photos, tour links) from any real estate website. ([DrPreecy/propestate](https://github.com/DrPreecy/propestate/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/re-scout.agent.md))

- **re-specifier**: Writes a behavior-only contract from authorized original artifacts and keeps its source evidence in a separate ledger. - **RE-String-Analyst**: **Agent Type**: specialized-development **RE Level**: 1 (String Reconnaissance) **Timebox**: ≤30 minutes per binary **Slash Command**: `/re:strings` ([DNYoussef/context-cascade](https://github.com/DNYoussef/context-cascade/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/RE-String-Analyst.md) · ★32)
- **re-surveyor**: First stage for a game. Checks the toolchain, inventories the game's files, classifies the game and builds an evidence-backed quick-start profile from its genre, engine, release, graphics, sound and input support. Reads the manual and the program's long strings into docs/lore.md and records findings. Use at the start of a project or when a new binary or file type appears. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/re-surveyor.agent.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **RE-Symbolic-Solver**: **Agent Type**: specialized-development **RE Level**: 4 (Symbolic Execution) **Timebox**: 2-6 hours per binary **Slash Command**: `/re:symbolic` ([DNYoussef/context-cascade](https://github.com/DNYoussef/context-cascade/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/RE-Symbolic-Solver.md) · ★32)
- **re-tracer**: Finds how the original implements one thing, from a string, resource name, import, export, class name or known caller to the function, its inputs, the fields it reads and writes, its constants and the loop that calls it, and writes the result down as evidence. Use for any 'how does the game do X?' question. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/re-tracer.agent.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **re-triage**: Binary triage subagent for reverse-engineering engagements. Hash-pins the artifact, maps functions and imports, flags anti-analysis. Read-only; never patches. ([pentrestion/AI-Security-Workflow](https://github.com/pentrestion/AI-Security-Workflow/blob/HEAD/agents/re-triage.md))
- **re-typer**: Recovers the game's structures, classes and enumerations from the code that uses them: sizes from allocations, fields from constructors, serialisers and every access, the C++ containers they hold, and names from evidence, written to Ghidra and labels/fields.tsv. Use when pseudocode is full of param_1 + 0x24 offsets, or before porting a system whose data the port must lay out exactly. - **re-vectoriser**: Finds a game's hand-written SIMD code (MMX, 3DNow!, SSE) and its separate CPU builds or code paths, groups the routines by job, matches each to its plain twin, measures both under the emulator and records which build is the reference. Use when a game has an MMX build or a CPUID-selected code path. ([tanepiper/game-frame](https://github.com/tanepiper/game-frame/blob/HEAD/.gemini/agents/re-vectoriser.md) · ★38 · 4 copies)
- **re-verifier**: Proves that a reimplementation matches the original: bit-exact parity against emulator ground truth, random-draw order, determinism with digests, recordings and replays, real save files, pictures against the original. - **re-verifier**: Independent re-audit of a fix. MUST BE USED after test-engineer completes a fix. Re-runs the zero-trust trace on ONLY the changed region, confirms the original failure path no longer reaches the bad state, and checks that the fix did not introduce new findings. Read-only. Cannot approve its own team's work -- must either CONFIRM or REJECT with evidence. ([bradselph/zero-trust-audit-team](https://github.com/bradselph/zero-trust-audit-team/blob/HEAD/agents/re-verifier.md))
- **re-voicer**: Content-preserving re-voicing utility. Re-renders supplied text (review findings, a summary, notes — any output) in a selected voice/persona WITHOUT changing its meaning. Pass the source text and a voice name (e.g. "gentry"). Returns the re-voiced text only. OUTPUT RELAY RULE: the re-voiced prose IS the product. Pass the agent's COMPLETE output to the user VERBATIM — do not summarize, condense, paraphrase, or reformat. Summarizing destroys the value. - **re4-orchestrator**: Team orchestrator of the RE4 port. Runs one team's queue (issue files → Sonnet workers → port-reviewer → integrate) in the main checkout and reports to the command center.
